587 +                 return false;
588 +             }
589 + 
590 +             // If forced window creation is active, skip the bundle check and
591 +             // `setActivationPolicy:` (which raises uncatchable ObjC exceptions
592 +             // on non-bundled executables).  We still call `finishLaunching` so
593 +             // that NSApp is in a usable state and NSWindows can be created.
594 +             if force_window_creation() {
595 +                 let _: () = msg_send![shared_app, finishLaunching];
596 +                 set_shared_nsapp(shared_app as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
597 +                 set_nsapp_initialized(true);
598 +                 eprintln!("[mac_window] init_nsapplication: forced mode (no bundle)");
599 +                 return true;
600 +             }
601 + 
602 +             // Detect whether we are running as a proper .app bundle with an
603 +             // Info.plist.  Non-bundled executables (e.g. `cargo test` binaries)
604 ~             // cannot safely call `setActivationPolicy:` or create NSWindows —
605 +             // these raise uncatchable NSInternalInconsistencyExceptions that
606 +             // cause immediate SIGABRT ("Rust cannot catch foreign exceptions").
607 +             //
608 +             // [`NSBundle mainBundle`] returns a bundle object even for non-bundled
609 +             // executables, but [`bundleIdentifier`] returns nil when there is no
610 +             // Info.plist.  Messaging nil in ObjC is safe (returns zero/nil), so
611 +             // this detection never throws.
612 +             let is_bundled = {
613 +                 let cls_bundle = match objc::runtime::Class::get("NSBundle") {
614 +                     Some(c) => c,
615 +                     None => return false,
616 +                 };
617 +                 let main_bundle: *mut objc::runtime::Object = msg_send![cls_bundle, mainBundle];
618 +                 if main_bundle.is_null() {
619 +                     false
620 +                 } else {
621 +                     let bid: *mut objc::runtime::Object = msg_send![main_bundle, bundleIdentifier];
622 +                     !bid.is_null()
623 +                 }
624 +             };
625 + 
626 +             if !is_bundled {
627 +                 return false;
628 +             }
629 + 
630 +             // Bundled process: safe to fully initialise AppKit.
631 +             let _: () = msg_send![shared_app, finishLaunching];
632 +             let _: () = msg_send![
633 +                 shared_app,
634 +                 setActivationPolicy: 0 /* NSApplicationActivationPolicyRegular */
635 +             ];
636 +

638 +             set_nsapp_initialized(true);
639 +             true
640 ~         })
